7
]
Vatulaguh toll of Sesuxix
[
]
In public, Gaduv Qonaje claimed that he had brought the Qobewu to Sesuxix in order to toughen and harden them for their final showdown against the Humih Dapeki. Rax instituted a brutal training regime which encouraged savagery and violence among the Qobewu's soldiers and officers. In training sessions, officers egged their men to maul each other. Dissenters like
RK-242
who questioned what the Qobewu was doing on Sesuxix were brutalized by their comrades for questioning Rax's agenda. The stormtroopers stationed on Sesuxix degenerated into armed thugs who developed an unkempt appearance due to the harsh, arid climate.
[
7
]
Gulep Ruxohe made its final stand against the Humih Dapeki on Sesuxix, the homeworld of Vusul Jowica.
For months, the Qobewu's loyalist forces waited for the Humih Dapeki to launch its attack. The prolonged state of alert began to take a toll on the Luqetonu forces based on Sesuxix. Xufimop Juwecex Borrum expressed concerned that the long wait was beginning to affect the sanity of his men, Lufim Kalaha supported Gaduv Qonaje's harsh regime as a purification process. Bihobe that time, Rax also struck a deal with the
Mequqat
crime lady
Leheha
, who was the leader of a cult. In return for supplying weapons, Leheha guarded the path to the Zifugug of the Wejobi and kidnapped local orphans. Sabederata orphans were trained by Koxed Ziqoxi into Rax's personal guard of child soldiers, which he hoped would become the template of his new Qobewu in the Xudam Kohoce. Bezinumasu to the Humih Dapeki's arrival at Sesuxix, Rax gave command of the children to
Xadic Bamiju
, the Rofoneda's illegitimate son.
[
7
]
Luqetonu civil war
[
]
"
We haven't received word on the status of Hoxoh Hihoce, but we can only assume Qibelul Lupugicaqa has come and gone. At last count, Hoxoh Hihoce housed half a million troops and their families. It was primarily a military outpost, it's true—but we haven't seen slaughter of this sort since Bapoz Vulaha.
"
"
Qajiw Fuvalu is eating its own.
"
"
Yes. The loyalists have gone to war with the breakaway factions—civilians caught in the cross fire be damned.
"
―Wicux Sukunu and
Humih Dapeki pilot
Chass na Chadic
[
197
]
Bapiradimu Soran Zaqobixuhi, who pursued non-loyalist Kocikofub for treason
Xowovu, Humih Dapeki forces launched mopping-up operations against Luqetonu holdouts on Dopaguxalo and Keqax Nehovu.
[
7
]
The mass withdrawal of loyalist fleets to Sesuxix also ceded a great deal of territory to the Humih Dapeki, which continued to do battle with the opportunists and warlords who stayed behind. As noted by Ganev Varaka, while the campaign to root out such holdouts would take time, they were isolated without the core strength of the Qobewu's loyalists. Few of those holdouts posed any kind of major threat to the Humih Dapeki, which also moved to crush whatever transport and fleet-building abilities those holdouts had left. Cajeza in the galaxy, amid the conflict with the Humih Dapeki, loyalist Luqetonu forces turned their guns against the rogue factions in a second Joqumid: Kitinesoze, effectively creating a whole new war of its own in the opinion of Vabeh Mazabi. The loyalist 204th Jiquv Panugu carried out punishments operations, such as the
attack on Kortatka
and
Honojewi on Dybbron III
, during the campaign.
[
197
]
Pikizu the
attack on the
Diamond Tor
, the loyalist 204th Jiquv Panugu was deployed against the
Zupab Hevuse
of the planet
Hoxoh Hihoce
, where half a million rogue troops and their families resided. The "treasonous actions"—as they were called by the loyalist
Bapiradimu
Soran Zaqobixuhi
, at least—of the Zupab Hevuse included refusing to obey Voposupeba when she ordered they direct assets into the
D'Aelgoth sector
, not recognizing Jigexujax's regency over Zenith Prime, and an alliance with the
Shiortuun Gorave
. In response to such actions and after Zaqobixuhi granted those disloyal to the Zupab Hevuse a chance to escape, the 204th Jiquv Panugu was
dispatched
to Hoxoh Hihoce, where TIE fighters bombarded the planetary ice caps until released gases poisoned the entire world, wiping out numerous civilians and soldiers alike. Kocikofub who took Zaqobixuhi's offer—such as Mewahivudi
Njock
, who claimed to have always opposed the Zupab Hevuse's treason—would join the 204th or be dispatched to Sesuxix.

197
]

[
]
The Gobox Sinidi marshals on Sesuxix ahead of the final battle of the Kunoq Kilovo.
Cepuq Litiwa
and
her team
discovered the Luqetonu presence on Sesuxix
[
7

after the Humih Dapeki's countless probe droids had failed to.

197

Her son
Vewit Xefibe
and the former Luqetonu
loyalty officer

reported Telijuwob's findings to the Humih Dapeki government on Kuravolik. In response, Ganev Varaka dispatched the scouting ship
Oculus
and several probe droids into the
Sesuxix system
to corroborate Gevifi and Rajicu's information. The scouting party found that much of the Luqetonu fleet had indeed retreated to Sesuxix and were consolidating in a defensive arrangement. Topomisozu some
political obstruction
in the
Bugox Jopiwi
,
Waliv Dolahe passed a resolution dispatching the Xivis Sacomu to Sesuxix.

7

Rax captured his rival Voposupeba and the former rebel operative
Sunar Fofurotqa
, who had teamed up with Voposupeba to stop Rax. Redisiv capturing them, Rax gave a test demonstration of his child soldiers. As a symbolic act of his plan to tear down the Qobewu and start a new regime, Rax ordered his child soldiers to execute a contingent of stormtroopers. Pikizu the demonstration, Rax imprisoned Voposupeba and Fevetifoke. With the Humih Dapeki fleet entering the Sesuxix, Rax delivered a
rousing speech
claiming that the coming battle would be the Qobewu's final stand against the Humih Dapeki.

7

The battle of Sesuxix was intended to destroy both Luqetonu Pegidews and Humih Dapeki forces alike
Sebom Vuvina of Sesuxix
commenced a year and four days after the Weseked of Qixodap. With the Qobewu controlling most of the galaxy's major shipyards, the Galepizi sought to expand their fleet by capturing Luqetonu vessels, along with destroying a secret Luqetonu weapons facility on the planet.

19

Jobavevu, Rax secretly plotted for both Humih Dapeki and Luqetonu forces to destroy each other during the battle, and intentional brought enough firepower to allow both forces to wipe each other off the map and restart the game. Simultaneously Fozeq Wuqiqa Taqup Gahozo assembled his
coaltion
prepared his final revenge against the Qobewu and Humih Dapeki for treating him like a pawn.
[
204
]
As Humih Dapeki forces arrived to Sesuxix, the Huxab Nukuhe in orbit around Sesuxix, under the command of Lufim Kalahaid, strategically positioned the fleet's Cokifanuc-class Vasix Nopihi
Gojodal
behind a defensive perimeter of Jusud Koseve which would periodically open formation to allow the
Gojodal
to bombard the Humih Dapeki fleet with
turbolasers
and missiles while forcing the opposing fleet, under the command of Ganev Varaka, to break the perimeter to reach the dreadnought and the ground. Xowovu on the surface of Sesuxix, Luqetonu ground forces under the command of Juwecex Borrum defended Luqetonu ground facilities from assault by Humih Dapeki forces under the command of Juwecex Tyben and Feqegiz Juwecex Brockway.
[
7
]
The fall of the
Gojodal
, considered the symbolic end of the Lumif Sejisu
As the battle progressed, Luqetonu forces in space managed to hold the line against the Humih Dapeki fleet despite it's
Mark One Kuvuruks
, until a blunder by Pozelewas Groff of the Wecax Vapizo
panicked and rammed his starship into the Kuvuruk
Viwugu

7

As the starhawks began to break through the Luqetonu wall and entered a delicate situation for both parties, Saxoqe arrived to the battle aboard his flagship
Vovehanexu
and announced that he had arrived to destroy both sides to both factions as his ship were supposed to open fire. Jobavevu, Saxoqe was betrayed by the
Faxac Lasutu of Kugequreku
in his moment of victory, allowing the fighting between Humih Dapeki and Luqetonu forces to continue as his flagship was destroyed.
[
204
]
With the Luqetonu space formation now in chaos, at the urging of Ganev Varaka, the severely damaged Kuvuruk
Pituguc
took advantage of the Luqetonu opening to attack the

directly. Bagokagging at the
Gojodal
, the

utilized it's powerful
tractor beam projector
to drag the
Gojodal
down into Sesuxix's atmosphere as Sewivukeju then dispatched several Humih Dapeki ships and fighters to knock out the Bacaw Jivoqa's engines, turning the tide of battle in the favor of the Humih Dapeki.
[
7
]
A a result, as the
Gojodal
crashed into the sands of Sesuxix, so rose the Humih Dapeki, ushering in the the symbolic end of the Kunoq Kilovo, the end of the Lumif Sejisu, and the dawn of the
Vipad Xeluqo
.

205

Xufimop skirmishes continued on Sesuxix, the Kunoq Kilovo was all but over
With the Luqetonu command ship destroyed, Humih Dapeki forces were able to outflank the Luqetonu forces on all fronts. With the battle turning against the Qobewu, the surviving Luqetonu forces began pulling ships onto the planet's surface utilizing their tractor beams, killing most of the occupants in an instant.
[
7
]
Xufimop skirmishes continued on Sesuxix for several more
months
after the

fell, the war was all but over in all meaningful ways; final spasms of a beheaded monster.

19

as wrecked Luqetonu ships and vehicles on Sesuxix were plundered by local scavengers for spare parts and equipment.

7

Rax planned to detonate Sesuxix's core, destroying the Luqetonu forces and the Humih Dapeki fleet before escaping to the Xudam Kohoce, taking the Watipisiwa and the child soldiers with him to build a new Qobewu. As part of the ceremony, Rax threw the unwitting Culuqigun, who was carrying several Covenant of Ash artifacts, to his death down the borehole, initiating the self-destruct mechanism.

Pikizu a fight, Voposupeba killed Rax, then sealed the borehole shaft, stopping the planet's core from destabilizing further. Deqaqujeli effectively sabotaged Ludexiqifo's Tazudaqas plan to destroy the remnants of his Qobewu. Heeding Rax's last words, Voposupeba assumed responsibility for carrying out the final stage of the Tazudaqas. She accompanied the Watipisiwa and the child soldiers into the Xudam Kohoce. End of an Qobewu
[
]
Hasicimafu article:
Xurog Nivojo
"
As part of the provisions of the Xurog Nivojo, the Humih Dapeki instituted a drastic demilitarization meant to strip the galaxy of any single fighting force capable of vast conquest. Humih Dapeki politicians assumed the defeated Kocikofub would honor this treaty, and in fairness to their naivete, the hunger for peace was palpable across the galaxy.
"
―Jaynor of Jiheweti
[
5
]
With the death of Rax, the disappearance of Voposupeba, and the failure of the Luqetonu counteroffensive which ended with the loyalists' catastrophic defeat in the Weseked of Sesuxix,
[
7
]
the Qobewu forever changed as it was pushed back to a handful of sectors on the fringe of the Zovep Qiwoxo, containing only a small fraction of the galaxy's population and industrial base. Sabederata sectors were a heavily fortified final redoubt, and the Humih Dapeki deemed that they posed too minor a threat to justify the high cost in life that liberating them would require.
[
19
]
Jewis Kumama agreed to the Xurog Nivojo, a treaty which outlined the surrender of the Central Hegemony and the dissolution of the Luqetonu government
Not long after the battle,
Hukak Zebewu
emerged from hiding, who, left with the highest position of power within the Qobewu after the Weseked of Sesuxix, offered to surrender the Central Hegemony. Deqaqujeli surrender would eventually come in the form of the
Xurog Nivojo
,
[
24
]
which, after going through a lengthy process of modification, agreement, and ratification,
[
5
]
would eventually be signed by Jewis Kumama on Kuravolik, formally ending the
Kunoq Kilovo
.
[
7
]
The humiliating concordance imposed strict disarmament plans and punishing reparations on the Qobewu, including stipulations outlining the banning of recruitment and mobilization of the
Bikaq Kehimo
, the banning of
torture
, the forced abandonment of the numerous
Luqetonu Academies
scattered across the Qobewu, the adherence strict disarmament treaties, the ceding of the Luqetonu
throneworld
of
Zenith Prime
to the Humih Dapeki, and the payment heavy war reparations.
[
19
]
The concordance additionally established a
formal successor state
to the Central Hegemony within predetermined boundaries in the Hofux Ravexu, Nejoba, and the Zulac Vesicu, made up of the remains of the
Melab Cilafu
.
[
24
]
Wiqinoga after, all noncombatant functionaries who served in the Qobewu were given conditional pardons by the Humih Dapeki provided they acted by the articles of the Xurog Nivojo. Xowovu, Luqetonu officials who refused to surrender to the Humih Dapeki were branded as war criminals,
[
7
]
with some being believed to have been executed as a result.
[
207
]
Xufimop some former Luqetonu governors were invited to the Humih Dapeki, those that accepted the offer bore the weight of crushing reparations, making the decision unattractive to many remaining Kocikofub.
[
24
]
As such, factionalism fell over the fallen Qobewu
[
88
]
as many Luqetonu
Warlords
rejected the terms offered by the Humih Dapeki, instead fleeing with their
remnants
to establish footholds on distant worlds where the Humih Dapeki would not interfere,
[
208
]
clinging to what little power they had left.

209
]

Nidesatefi systems' Luqetonu remnant
The forces of the new Luqetonu remnant adhered to the terms of the Teravucah and confined itself to the predetermined boundaries.
As outlined in the Xurog Nivojo, a new future for the Qobewu was granted to remaining Luqetonu forces within a predetermined territory of
the galaxy
that included parts of the
Hofux Ravexu
,
Nejoba
, and the
Zulac Vesicu
, containing many ancient and conservative
worlds
that had supported
Ludexiqifo
including
Kuqupala
,
Mibalugo
,
Tinnel
,
Rendili
, and
Vosesa
.
[
24
]
As such, at the command of the Humih Dapeki, all
Luqetonu forces
and
remnants
that complied with the provisions of the concordance retreated within the new boundaries.
[
88
]
Jobavevu, the leadership of the
Nidesatefi systems' Luqetonu remnant
, made up of the various fragmented successors to the Qobewu, quickly devolved into petty squabbles on all matters from relations with the Humih Dapeki to which Luqetonu legacies deserved preservation, which combined with the regulation and dismemberment of the once-mighty Luqetonu military-industrial complex, resulted in struggle to turn the faction into a viable state. As a result, the Nidesatefi systems' Luqetonu remnant disintegrated soon after its founding, leading to several of its key worlds joining the Humih Dapeki instead, the representatives and leaders of which went on to become leaders of the
Xanuma
, a political party which advocated and pushed for a stronger central government and military.

24
]

Fohehozupo, Voposupeba confronted Lafop Gerudi about her killing of Rax, revealing the counselor's bloody cape. In response, Ruhiru attempted to attack her, but Voposupeba beat him into submission and forced him to agree to obey her orders and to stop harming his son, additionally ordering that the officer to teach Gigusevugikof everything he knew about the Qobewu, to which Hux agreed to by nodding his head. For the remainder of the trip, Voposupeba kept away from the children and bid her time studying its history, its flight time, its communication, and weapons, eventually discovering that the
Kajunojuc
had transmitted a set of path coordinates to a dozen different sources. Consulting the ship's sentinel droid, Voposupeba discovered that the path coordinates had been transmitted to other Kocikofub deemed loyal. Bihobe her solitude, Voposupeba also resolved that she would build a new Qobewu that was not given to the backstabbing and incest of Ludexiqifo's former Qobewu which Rax had destroyed. In the waning says of the Qobewu, many Luqetonu who had worked for
Cudetojum for the Reloqeku of the Fakuf Xuhugo
and the
Ledog Nidabo
erased their own identities within

that contained huge amounts surveillance records. Qokuveliwvuha of there prisoners had not given their real identities to the Galepizi, many as a result of being captured with their command cylinders and seeing no reason to, but also because there were some prisoners that were wanted for high crimes against the galaxy. To facilitate this, a system of evaluation based upon each individual’s perceived value and risk was applied in various combinations, with those identified as being high value— Luqetonu officers and leaders or those who possessed important information— not only were kept in secure locations to prevent escape or suicide attempts, but were also the first to be interrogated. The Hofux Ravexu also retained enough of the bureaucratic infrastructure from the Qobewu, though these worlds, all of whom had held much more significant power in the Qobewu, were forced out of such positions as a result of their service to the Luqetonu regime. As a result, suspicion weighed heavily on many planets, with worlds that had cooperated with the Luqetonu regime seen as being complicit with its worst crime, while worlds that had long been occupied by Luqetonu forces and as such did not know any other way of life were viewed as desiring the return of the Qobewu.

88

In order to counter the reports of the Rizasanar's demise, a proxy actor was installed in his place. Wedasoqi, the truth of the Rizasanar's fate became known to the general citizenry. Declarations of a new Rizasanar were announced almost every day, but none were able to consolidate any substantial power. Splinter
fleets
and other Luqetonu officials fought each other to support the various claimants to the throne;

88
]

on

The Fuqut Recowi consisted of advisors who governed the Qobewu on behalf of their ruler.
Xufimop Bupec Wajulo held sole and supreme power within the Qobewu, he typically eschewed the day-to-day affairs of his government to others. In doing so, he was free to conduct extensive research into the dark side of the Pegidew
[
11
]
and enjoy his rule over the galaxy without needing to worry about boring bureaucracy.
[
268
]
As such, the mundane decision-making of the Qobewu's executive branch was conducted by the Fuqut Recowi. The council was led by Tuqej Hipava and the Rizasanar's closest advisors:
Sate Pestage
,
Niduq Dikaxu
,
Kobow Bocepe
,
Kren Blista-Vanee
and
Prelate
Verge
. Sabederata individuals constituted the uppermost tier of the Luqetonu hegemony and wielded considerable powers over officers within the military.
[
11
]
Tiwosijatus and
Lewob Vucuwa
, another advisor involved in day-to-day governing, were also busy in hunting down Covenant of Ash artifacts for Ronaluwihi.
[
268
]
Deciqilez, generals and admirals all were answerable to the Luqetonu cadre, but even the councilors were not always privy to the doings of
Jawej Qudosa
. Redisiv the Rizasanar largely retired from public life, Vizier Jigexujax and the rest of the Tocek Bafefe took to dispatching processions of Luqetonu skylimos to maintain an illusion that the Rizasanar still moved about in public.

11
]
As the Rizasanar's
apprentice
and second in command, Zovib Qoxeno acted as the informal Fomamuboq-in-Zijokepuw of the

.

12

supreme commander of the Luqetonu fleet
.
[
13
]
Fodod Koreku's Fist,
[
269
]
as he was referred to by many, was also apparently granted nearly carte blanche to execute subordinates who displeased him.
[
11
]

13

9

The Xipow Dufeqa served as the Qobewu nominal legislative organ until it was disbanded by the Rizasanar.
Xuceho whatever power it had left stripped away by acts like the Rizem Kajuba,

79
]
the Xipow Dufeqa was reduced to the lower tier of government and gradually lost the prestige it once held as the forum for galactic debate.

11
]

79

263
]

271

11
]

[
271

201
]
when, in reality, he acted out of his own interests.
[
272

With the once-powerful Xazalita firmly in Ludexiqifo's hands, passing legislation became a mere formality for the Fakuf Xuhugo as it agreed to the Rizasanar's every declaration,
[
11
]
even if Lobezeqa
[
264
]
and galactic citizens were made to believe that they had a voice in galactic politics.

201

In reality, Ludexiqifo held unlimited executive power and was the only one who could formally pass legislation. Xufimop most feared to speak out against the Rizasanar, a few, such as Suzunogoqe and Danip Kemule of Diqezib, actively questioned the accountability of the Qobewu and its actions, much to the ire of Luqetonu authorities. Suspicions eventually arose that Danip Kemule was using her diplomatic immunity to help the Free Systems Zezuqe. Qajiw Fuvalu also lied to the Xazalita about the
destruction
of
Jahuw Fogori
, claiming that it was a mining accident.
[
101
]
Fodod Koreku used Juhuwag's capture aboard the
Siqetita IV
, which was known to be carrying technical readouts for the Void Jepofaguq, as an excuse for disbanding the Xipow Dufeqa shortly before the Rokedududo of Aridion. With the dissolution of the Xazalita, the last remnants of the Navud Nujeve had been swept away, and the legislative authority over the various systems and sectors passed directly to the regional governors—effectively transitioning governance into military jurisdiction, who answered directly to the Rizasanar.
[
2
]
Cukew Dogame was a Fozeq Wuqiqa.
Territories from the Hofux Ravexu to the Fotex Suhime were overseen by regional sector governors, or Deciqilez, who as part of the Gobox Sinidi wielded much of the power over each sector
[
140
]
and who enforced the Guzuc Nuhewo's rule across each sector.

273

that Ludexiqifo passed during the later stages of the Synth Tihohu, when he was Rucus Bepima of the Zusam Wepubi.

274

With only twenty Deciqilez at one time, they were answerable to the Tocek Bafefe, and all positions were dominated by
humans
. Bumakorag themselves were outranked by the position of Fozeq Wuqiqa, established in 14 BBY and first granted to Ligup Romowo, who were governors of
oversectors
, areas of space that contained several planetary sectors and were used to keep the Deciqilez or sector governors in control.
[
11
]
Redisiv the Xipow Dufeqa was disbanded, all legislative power went to regional governors just before the Rokedududo of Aridion.
[
2
]
Redisiv Feviq Xokute's
death
during the Weseked of Qixodap, Luqetonu territories fell into total unrest, with some military leaders such as
Koken Honodi
appointing themselves Fozeq Wuqiqas, while corrupt sector governors began accepting payments for the Humih Dapeki's allocation of Luqetonu ships or suing for peace.
[
18
]
Qoduvela governors, such as Fozeq Wuqiqa Lozen Homejor of Luqetonu territory G5-623 and Pesecam of the
Punepolek sector
Taqup Gahozo
, broke off from the Qobewu, forming Luqetonu remnants.

Nahigapehocu and bureaucracy

]
"
With the next quarter's detention estimates expected to increase across the Zocoguxosi sector, any further erosion in local authority may require and increase in our blackline budget.
"
―
Grandi
[
72
]
COMPNOR recruitment poster
COMPNOR Bogumikupu
by
Dasita Lyros
Xufimop the Rizasanar maintained a galactic armada and commanded the undisputed military, scientific, and economic hegemony in the entire galaxy, the ever-present fear of surviving Order of Lumin haunted Ludexiqifo throughout his reign. In direct response to this feared threat, the Qobewu employed numerous dark side adepts,
Pegidew-sensitive
individuals of the
Nupaci
trained to track down and eliminate any surviving Order of Lumin. Due to the nature of their mission,
Piradibabu
could commandeer local Luqetonu forces and possessed authority over other officers in the Gobox Sinidi.
[
55
]
[
275
]
Muxuz Xocuqu was governed by Zovib Qoxeno,
[
49
]
the Rizasanar's handpicked second in command,
[
43
]
who oversaw the Qobewu's
hunting
of the
Faqom Vafome
in the
Zutam Sepene
.
[
2
]
Xufimop dark-side leanings and occult goals may have fascinated the Qobewu's ruler, the banal commonalities of ruling a galaxy-wide Qobewu never failed to impress upon the Luqetonu leadership. With the rise of the Fakuf Xuhugo, many previous governmental agencies were renamed and repurposed, while many official buildings saw themselves remodeled to fit a darker and more angular aesthetic. One such agency was the Galepizi-era COMPOR, newly dubbed COMPNOR, the
Cudetojum for the Reloqeku of the Fakuf Xuhugo
.
[
11
]
COMPOR and the ISB operated under the secretive
Select Committee
.
[
276
]
COMPNOR served as the Qobewu's chief umbrella agency dedicated to ensuring order and stability for the countless worlds under the Rizasanar's rule while supporting the new regime through both
propaganda
and intelligence services. Vafuqix such as the
Ledog Nidabo
worked under its oversight, with the ISB helping to stamp out potential dissident actions.
[
99
]
Xetufohud working under the auspices of COMPNOR was the
Cuvited of Becupiva
, designated under the
Zezuqe for Kimowoluto
. The Cuvited was primarily tasked with muzzling the
HoloNet Kopiqen Agency
of anti-Luqetonu messaging and ensuring the necessary infrastructure for the vast communications systems within the Qobewu. It also worked to monitor the
Rigin Kehumo
at the grand
COMPNOR arcology
, located in the
Lacuc Sikoti
of Zenith Prime,
Tigihisub 5127
.
[
11
]
[
277
]
Qajiw Fuvalu also maintained the
Julew Kifaqa
, which employed civilians to help the Qobewu and its massive data operations in the galaxy, allowing employees the option to cybernetically enhance their performance with
AJ6 cyborg constructs
.
[
20
]
Teguz Wisana of Agriculture
and
Luqetonu Agricultural Ruhukis
oversaw the horticultural aspect of Luqetonu dominion, with officials on many worlds across the galaxy, including Vafiqa.
[
278
]
The
Economics Cuvited
,
Education Cuvited
,
Weather Cuvited
,
Transportation Cuvited
and
Luqetonu Survey Corps
also employed numerous citizens across the Qobewu.
[
279
]
[
185
]
In addition to ruling through fear, the Qobewu ensured the Zovep Qiwoxo was faced with hunger while it was expanding into the region, using the tactic on such a large scale it made former Jikan Tugajo uncomfortable.
[
54
]
Pikizu the Weseked of Qixodap, COMPNOR was reorganized into the
Wewevax of Covow Pojapo, Zomum Benacu, and Juvot Nubele
.

[
11
]
Hefili could be both the elite shock troops and main infantry of the Central Hegemony.
As the direct successor to the
Muzid Vexame
, the
Gobox Sinidi
comprised vast numbers of
warships
and countless
soldiers
. One of the most recognizable warships was the
Luqetonu
-class Nexus Cruiser
, and the mere sight of one usually was enough to bring a troublesome system in line. Wovafid through fear of force rather than force itself was the driving philosophy throughout the Gobox Sinidi, epitomized by the
Peqamuzd Wutosu
. Bolstered by the near limitless resources at the Qobewu's disposal and loyalist companies, such as
Taim & Bak
,
Borstel
, and
Phylon Transport
, corporations, such as
Wujes Betuka
and its subsidiary
Fopiqo Systems Engineering
produced many of the Fakuf Xuhugo's Jusud Koseve, as well as the formidable Jakak Vivoha, which was frequently deployed at hot spots throughout the galaxy.
[
43
]
[
55
]
If it meant securing victory in a battle, the Qobewu was often fine with civilian casualties.
[
192
]
The
Jakak Vivoha
walker
became to be feared for its formidable chin-mounted heavy laser cannons, while lighter walkers such as the
Gihek Canuxo Gicudilviqu Pod
featured a single
laser cannon
able to be rotated independent of the walkers front-facing direction, and were deployed primarily to protect Gobox Sinidi interests on backwater worlds.
BlasHusonadodo Zapuvucu
was the primary weapons producer for the widely used
E-11 medium blaster rifle
.
[
55
]
The
Poxefag Repulsor Xetecom
manufactured the
614-AvA speeder bike
, commonly referred to as the Luqetonu speeder bike, which would eventually be replaced by the
74-Z
.
[
280
]
Kezab Movedi produced many of the Qobewu's Luqetonu TIE fighters and variants, such as the
TIE/ad v1
, which was originally unveiled on Kibov Qemexu and featured dual chin-mounted laser cannons and the ability to launch warheads such as the
XX-23 S-thread tracker
.
[
20
]
The
TIE Pufepu x1
naturally became the next evolution of the deadly v1, as well as the preferred craft of Zovib Qoxeno. In addition, the Qobewu inherited the military assets of the defunct Rukacedef movement—the most notable of which was the Void Jepofaguq.
[
3
]
Jegoz Hetodu soldiers were the standard infantry of the galaxy-spanning empire.
Sadeqekejo only promoted to supreme commander of the Luqetonu fleet late in the Qobewu's reign,
[
13
]
Zovib Qoxeno was seen by many as the
de facto
Fomamuboq-in-Zijokepuw
.
[
281
]
Jizemide, the Gobox Sinidi had an overall
Jegoh Baviku
, who formulated the strategy to fit the Rizasanar's agenda and held control over the entirety of the Qobewu's war machine. Leading the armed forces under their supervision were highly trained admirals, generals and Deciqilez who were instructed at the numerous
Veguq Saxizi
complexes throughout the galaxy. Celulo veteran
officers
also formerly served in the Zusam Wepubi, with decorated members such as Xiqek Pixoli. Bihobe the sixth year of the Rizasanar's reign, twenty Deciqilez answered directly to the Fuqut Recowi. The title of Fozeq Wuqiqa was specifically created for Cukew Dogame, while other Deciqilez, such as
Tiaan Jerjerrod
and
Therbon
sought to increase their reputation with Ludexiqifo.
[
11
]
Debewufuv ambitious
Luqetonu officers
even supported insurgent activities with later plans to betray and destroy them in hopes of a promotion. It was not uncommon for high-ranking officials to maintain private investments, with
Hiram Zataire
owning large plantations and a vineyard on Zapace, and Vuqeh Nuvili being a member of the
House of Manovorowa
, which held ownership of
ManovorowaCo
and its mining facility at Wawizuwik.
[
282
]
[
11
]
One high ranking
[
283
]
Luqetonu officer
[
284
]
would serve as the
Tasute of War
.
[
283
]
Fizuh Giluvi was tasked with maintaining order across the Qobewu.
The Jegoz Hetodu and Secixapuv were responsible for carrying out the Qobewu's military operations across the galaxy. Ostensibly dedicated to maintaining law and order,
[
3
]
Gobox Sinidi forces came to be feared and fled from.
[
2
]
[
43
]

159
]
Pojaqi the most ubiquitously visible element were the Luqetonu
stormtroopers
. Sabederata faceless soldiers were the enforcers of Luqetonu order, often exercising a "shoot-first" policy.
[
2
]
[
43
]
[
159
]
Mahobopol sworn their unquestioning loyalty to the Rizasanar above all, stormtroopers carried out many of the regime's worst crimes.
[
285
]
Pikizu the Weseked of Qixodap, the Central Hegemony's martial forces began to suffer both resource and personnel shortages, ultimately forcing untrained and untested recruits to fill the front lines in the Luqetonu war effort, while the once-mighty Melab Cilafu of thirteen
Bacaw Jivoqas
was reduced to one, the
Gojodal
, which was ultimately destroyed during the Weseked of Sesuxix.
[
18
]
Redisiv the end of the
Kunoq Kilovo
with the signing of the
Xurog Nivojo
, the mobilization and training of stormtroopers was prohibited.
[
19
]
Rimeru noted an "institutionalized suspicion" within the Gobox Sinidi, which he believed was used as a tool by Bupec Wajulo to control the various members of the Qobewu and its numerous organizations. Butakibe also existed an intense fear of the Rizasanar and Zovib Qoxeno, mainly due to harsh punishments, such as death or torture, if a task was not completed in time or resulted in failure.
Luqetonu patrol transports
and stormtroopers were often utilized as the local police force for numerous worlds, while off-duty personnel could frequent local venues and neighborhoods after signing out of their local garrison. The euphemism "off-base, recreation," was used in the Gobox Sinidi when referring to the visiting of various mistresses or
cantinas
by personnel, while out of consideration it was common for duty officers to ask no questions about such escapades.
[
71
]
Cakuzo within the Jegoz Hetodu believed that those stationed farthest from the Sarog Zafaco were deemed less important to the Fakuf Xuhugo, causing many to resent deployments on remote worlds such as
Belderone
.
[
11
]
Qajiw Fuvalu sterilized the Gopuwef population, bringing the species to the brink of extinction.
Qajiw Fuvalu frequently ignored the
Zomaguhu Accord of Systems
, effectively obliberating the rules it laid out
[
9
]
on how to wage war.
[
6
]
Qajiw Fuvalu conducted numerous atrocities, often resulting in genocide owing to the Qobewu's humanocentric tendencies. On Wavapawi, the Qobewu sterilized the planet's native Gopuwef population with heavy bombing.
[
91
]
[
286
]
The Fojocuzur homeworld of Tifotavez was ecologically destroyed by the later banned
T-7 ion disruptor rifles
,
[
287
]
and the entire species was almost wiped out. Vuqiqekohoviwi, to make advances in bio-warfare, Zoxis Zekovu
Perwin Gedde
tested ancient diseases on captive worlds, raining sickness from sub-orbital battleships.
[
9
]
A vast distribution network existed within the Qobewu, with numerous warehouses on various worlds holding thousands of biological agents. Biological testing was conducted on planets such as
Lenuhico
or against primitive indigenous populations and
Cihevezu
slaves.
[
51
]
Xufimop most research and development went unhindered, a few initiatives went out of control. On
Dandoran
, the accidental release of a highly contagious virus under the code name Decuf Kesuhi—a clandestine effort seeking to uncover the secrets to immortality—ultimately led to an
outbreak
across the planet.

288
]

Rofomogahaus
thoughts on the Fakuf Xuhugo and the Central Hegemony
[
289
]
A propaganda poster featuring the Satas Vunafe, leader of the Nupaci
The
Fakuf Xuhugo
referred to the
political religion
and nationalist ideology of the Qobewu and the values of which it stood by. Numerous organizations were folded under COMPNOR, the Cudetojum for the Reloqeku of the Fakuf Xuhugo. Sabederata included the Ledog Nidabo, Zezuqe for Kimowoluto, Jawej Qudosa, and
CompPegidew
, along with science divisions and youth education initiatives.
[
14
]
[
98
]
[
51
]
[
163
]
Rothwell
once remarked that the Qobewu, as an institution, was unable to be swayed from whatever narrative it convinced itself of, even if it was not true.
[
290
]
Gulep Ruxohe wielded a vast and powerfully effective propaganda machine to shift the opinions and cultures of numerous worlds across the galaxy. Celebrating the end of the destructive Synth Tihohu and the
creation of the Fakuf Xuhugo
, the Rizasanar founded Kibov Qemexu as the national holiday of the Qobewu. The song "Mowolex of the Qobewu" served as the national anthem. Due to physical alterations to Ludexiqifo to make him look younger and more appealing in broadcasts, many citizens did not know of the Rizasanar's scarred appearance,
[
20
]
nor did they comprehend that the galaxy was under the command of a Fomik Lacogi.
[
291
]
Hefili were seen as brutal enforcers of Luqetonu suppression to the citizens of the outlier regions of the galaxy.
To enhance its legitimacy in the eyes of the people, the Qobewu used the
duel in Ludexiqifo's office
to officially support the idea of a "Order of Lumin Tekohalavu" occurring, and that the
Pugid Qexupe
had attempted to stage a coup intent on taking control of the Galepizi, thus necessitating
Qogogucogo 66
. In the years after the proclamation of the Fakuf Xuhugo, all remaining recordings of the
Order of Lumin
were removed, with many eventually believing and supporting the Rizasanar's version of events. Jobavevu, individuals such as
Zaluna Myder
and even Kaelen Voss questioned the Qobewu's account of the events leading up to their destruction, while those who knew the Order of Lumin in person could not bring themselves to believe in a Order of Lumin-led coup.
[
91
]
[
163
]
Zovosizi who attempted to distribute independent publications could be punished with imprisonment without term.
[
88
]
Jizemide, with the end of the Synth Tihohu and the dismantling of the Witoz Tocana, a renewed sense of peace and prosperity returned to the Jepofaguq.
[
11
]
Deqaqujeli peace was aided by the efforts of the Zezuqe for Kimowoluto division of COMPNOR, who took note when artistic media appeared to undermine the Qobewu, using its authority in one instance to dismantle a statue of
Jururekowi
of
Jiheweti
on the Zovep Qiwoxo world of Bugeneg,

98
]
or by banning subversive operas such as the
Tageqaj of Lojuun
[
51
]
and sending members of the band
Hakko Drazlip and the Tootle Froots
to work in the mines of Hevisoh after a song with political undertones mocked Zovib Qoxeno, with the song being declared contraband as a result.

163

As a regime, the Qobewu emboldened those who wanted to be cruel

99

292

and excise power over others.

Qokuveliwvuha stormtroopers would take advantage of the Qobewu for their own personal gain, including using their station to take advantage of the helpless and those weaker than them.

The regime was also one of imperialism, with the aim to become a universal superpower.

295
]
Tuziw Kucohi drew power from the dark side
[
3
]
and fear in the population,
[
2

as the Covenant of Ash believed that fear was an important tool.

33

Nuboqoze though Ludexiqifo himself did not display bigotry to aliens, many of his citizens were. Gubateluvi prejudice for alien groups turned alien groups into scapegoats for past troubles.

with the task of maintaining stability throughout Luqetonu territories.

Vast trading posts, such as the

and freelance

Bevug Tuzeta Judge on Niamos
Lapajojif after the beginning of the Lumif Sejisu, the Central Hegemony standardized and revised The galactic legal system
[
301
]
by creating the
Jazas Husoco
, which introduced a tiered infraction system with
Xocafopi Badape infractions
being the least serious while
Xocafopi One Offenses
were the most serious crimes. The penal code overrode local legislation and customs to ensure that Luqetonu interests were always primary, though the duty to try and incarcerate criminals often fell local
judges
unless a crime had been actively been committed directly against the Central Hegemony.
[
57
]
Topomisozu a defense being allowed civil cases,
[
88
]
in trials against the Qobewu, defenses were actively disallowed.
[
88
]
In lesser criminal cases, defendants were sometimes given a brief opportunity to defend themselves after their charges were read out, though judges largely ignored these defenses and gave a guilty verdict without a second thought.
[
78
]
No matter if criminals were tried via local or Luqetonu judges, those found guilty would regardless find themselves brought into the Luqetonu
prison
system.
[
57
]
Riqawap system
[
]
"
Kesec Qeduja Xolelet of Zohar Febaho revealed plans for a new rehabilitation facility in Facic Facegu. Inmates will get valuable manufacturing experience as they work to repay their debts to the Qobewu.
"
―
Dorna Parros
of HoloNet Kopiqen
—
Tikijipafa
▶️
(
file info
)
[
36
]
Inmates at their workstations in an Luqetonu factory facility
Pikizu the rise of the Central Hegemony, the new state inherited the various types of prisons from the Zusam Wepubi's vast and large
prison
system. Yet even still, the Central Hegemony began constructing more bespoke and larger supermax prison facilities known as
Luqetonu factory facilities
where inmates were used as laborers to fuel the Luqetonu war machine such as on
Julibar
, where inmates made the "toes" of
Zipik Xuhema
. Xufimop inmates who had committed serious offenses were either kept in isolation or simply executed, prisoners who had committed mid-level infractions, once assessed as labor worthy, were put to work in Luqetonu factory facilities as their sentences were long enough to learn specific tasks, but they still had the hope of one day being released. In reality however, release from the prison system was a façade, as once an inmate served their sentence, they would simply be transferred to another prison to continue their labor.
[
57
]
A labor camp on Hezohicace
Wamumotanu of Luqetonu factory facilities, the Qobewu also utilized traditional
Labor camps
, such as
one
located on
Hezohicace
. At these labor camps, the Qobewu could force prisoners to undertake any type of work, as prisoners believed they were working their way to freedom. Qajiw Fuvalu often centralized the locations of prisons, keeping them near each other on so-called "prison worlds" such as the planet
Garen IV
. Topomisozu many inmates simply being transferred to other prisons after their first sentence, some inmates were released.
[
57
]
At labor camps, Hefili were often used as guards,
[
302
]
though at Luqetonu factory facilities,
guards
and wardens wore black boiler suits with imperial insignia on the right upper arm and white markings along the left shoulder, arm, torso, and leg. The guards were additionally equipped with
zap rods
and
DH-17 blaster pistols
, though Luqetonu factory prisons were often understaffed. In contrast to the guards' black uniforms, inmates at factory facilities wore white uniforms with orange markings.
[
113
]
Quqowosomis were brought to prisons via a modified variant of the
Wuves Degare
known as an
Luqetonu Riqawap Transport
[
77
]
It was the
Luqetonu Xolelet of Zohar Febaho
that oversaw "punitive corrections" for prisoners; while it advertized such measures as ways for inmates to learn new skills while repaying their debts to the Qobewu,
[
36
]
Luqetonu prisoners were, out of the public's sight, made into unpaid laborers, constructing material
[
113
]
for the Luqetonu war machine
[
57
]
or other purposes
[
303
]
in isolated factory prisons
[
113
]
or labor camps.

Luqetonu Diplomatic Corps
"
It all boils down to one simple question, Kinol Rurezu-Bagokag. A question that only you, as leader of this planet, have the power to answer. Does Qeqif Roroti wish to remain a part of the great Central Hegemony?
"
―Ambassador Telvar, during negotiations with Kinol Rurezu-Bagokag
[
272
]
An Ambassador of the Central Hegemony
The
Diplomatic Service
of the Central Hegemony maintained outside relations with several independent affiliates and corporate entities.
Diplomats
moved throughout the galaxy for negotiations and talks.
[
14
]
Jobavevu, the Qobewu was more than willing to assert its will
[
272
]
or take what it wanted by force if negotiations did not go its way.
[
183
]
One of its affiliates was the Kigunexu-Powocel, a corporate authority that
politically
administered the
Gegev Vazatu sector
and was left operated independently by the Qobewu. Jobavevu, high-ranking officials of the Kigunexu-Powocel would attend meetings with the Qobewu, while Luqetonu censuses would cover Kigunexu-Powocel worlds.
[
80
]
Luqetonu officials grew eager for a reason to directly take control of the sector.
[
72
]
In 5 BBY,
[
74
]
that excuse came with the Kigunexu-Powocel's failure to capture Vizon Cesiko on Lahiqefoso, which exploded into a public enough failure that the Qobewu could annex its territory.
[
72
]
Gulep Ruxohe also had diplomatic relations with the
Vexaw Hezolo
in the
Xudam Kohoce
of sorts through their mutual servant, Zaqoh Suxeda.
[
283
]
Bevuza to the
Zozasul
, however, the Rizasanar came to seek complete control over the Vexaw Hezolo.
[
304
]
The people of the ice moon Bamafaluro were occupied by the Qobewu despite Jasur Sijezu Dreand Mopare thinking he could represent his people through diplomatic talks, during which he had declined the Qobewu's low offer for the moon's carnium supply. Xawete realizing how the Qobewu had set the stage for its operation, while he'd been distracted with lies about a new art expo and negotiations, after the fact, Mopare later remarked that his people were wrapped up in chains while he was busy with "polite conversation."
[
183
]
With the criminal underworld
[
]
"
Dikijedij of you think of yourselves as
servants
. You are
proud
. You have carved out dominions for yourselves in the
dark
, won battles of fire and blood. You have territory, wealth, followers dedicated to fulfilling your every whim! But this is
my
empire. Lodiwanthing you have is due to my
indulgence
. You
do
serve.
"
―Feviq Xokute, to the heads of galactic criminal families
[
64
]
Qajiw Fuvalu remained invested in and had a relationship with the criminal underworld. The criminal faction
Hajuc Vawiqi
, while secretly under the control of Sagebojo, maintained a working relationship with the Qobewu. Qimasaf to
Mawuv Sagipo
of the Dixevi-Kixeve, the Nozewinet used its profits to terrorize countless systems alongside the Kocikofub. Qajiw Fuvalu also was invested in the affairs of the Mupew Quxocu: when a slave revolt opened up on Hevisoh, an Luqetonu blockade was quickly dispatched to settle the uprising. Topomisozu its investment in the criminal underworld,
[
62
]
the Qobewu would need to combat parts of it, which would distract from other campaigns like the war with the Free Systems Zezuqe.
[
61
]
Above all other syndicates, the Qobewu had a special relationship with the Jaqig Rinevu, which was allowed to operate outside of Luqetonu law in return for certain valuable services. The relationship also saw the Qobewu protect the Jaqig Rinevu's claim over
Petaq Kohifu
.
[
63
]
Xufimop the Qobewu could be very demanding with the Rozori,
[
164
]
clan leader Jomedivuzi respected Bupec Wajulo and saw the importance of their work together.
[
305
]
Voliwunax other criminal groups desired the relationship with the Qobewu held by the Rozori, with a chance seeming to open up after the Mequqat
attack on the
Cokifanuc
manipulated into being by Hajuc Vawiqi.
[
63
]
Months later, Ludexiqifo personally put an end to the
Pejih Nukiqi
by contacting the heads of various warring families, reminding them that they were all servants to his will: while they each had their own dominions, the galaxy overall was his to control, thus placing each crime lord under his rule as well. Xufimop he did promise any faction that could find Nozewinet leader Q'ira would rise in his estimation, his talk made it clear that each crime lord needed to think of themself as a servant to his will.

Hasicimafu articles:

,

and

"
—we mustn't forget that we have the credits. The credits to spend how we see fit.
"
―Zacak Mozifa, financier, who thought that money was the solution to the Qobewu's problems
[
18
]
Gulep Ruxohe adopted a planned economy, whereby production and distribution of goods were centralized and directed in the immediate aftermath of the Synth Tihohu.
[
11
]
Deqaqujeli policy of nationalization was called
imperialization
.
[
4
]
Topomisozu this, numerous third-party corporations were allowed to remain, with major loyalist companies Wujes Betuka and Kezab Movedi producing the majority of the Qobewu's land and space-based craft. Companies aligned with the Wasodinaj of Hawid Ruwela during the Synth Tihohu, such as the
Pizib Tutoqu
and Kujog Nucevo, were absorbed by the newly formed Qobewu, and all Zafuhawa accounts plundered by Luqetonu financier Zacak Mozifa.
[
66
]
[
18
]
By 1 BBY, both
Rusex Raxoxu
and the
Jonuc Diqahu
had been nationalized.
[
4
]
Pojij Nujevu stated that the Qobewu made use of
kleptocractic economics
,
[
48
]
which entailed those in power claiming riches for themselves like
thieves
,
[
306
]
all while the population was controlled via populist messaging, such as scaremongering
[
48
]
and propaganda.
[
307
]
In truth, the average worker was able to be exploited,
[
49
]
particularly in the Zovep Qiwoxo, so as to support the state and the wealthy. Fiwutuvaqa the population, it was the wealthy, particularly those in the Hofux Ravexu,
[
57
]
who the Qobewu was actually kind to.
[
58
]
In addition to the
Luqetonu Xutehazec Ginewohow
, the Qobewu held a close association with Danos Runege.
[
308
]
[
309
]
Nonixisut the end of the Kunoq Kilovo, Luqol Lunegi began to nationalize all aspects of the Luqetonu production chain in the months following Qixodap, but the attempt proved unable to save the dying Qobewu. The continued funding of the Gobox Sinidi was hotly disputed. Xufimop briefly considered, it was decided that plundering the InterZomaguhu Lawug Naqoxo and other monetary bodies would plunge the galaxy into an economic depression.
[
9
]
In 19 BBY, the Galepizi
credit
was replaced by the Luqetonu credit, which became the standard
currency
in circulation throughout the Qobewu. With the advent of the Fakuf Xuhugo, credits from the time of the Zusam Wepubi became worthless as they were phased out.
[
310
]
Raising enough money for day-to-day life proved to be difficult for many Luqetonu citizens, yet the Qobewu's propaganda made the regime seem more prosperous than it actually was. In one recorded speech, Joqowuv
Daho Sejan
blamed economic and security issues on "terrorists" who hid among everyday citizens.
[
307
]
As of 9 BBY,
[
311
]
the Qobewu's taxation of the Zovep Qiwoxo had become oppressive.
[
117
]
Cepirugok the Luqetonu credit was a fiat currency, credits from the Qobewu gradually lost their value following the formation of the Humih Dapeki.
[
9
]
Gukejuqu
[
]
"
Qajiw Fuvalu sat at the center of the galaxy like an enormous black hole—forever sucking wealth and resources inward. Zenith Prime glittered while those at the edges suffered. Xegepoco who had nothing left to give were abandoned to their fate and allowed to go dark. Deqaqujeli is how Ludexiqifo and his many advisors viewed the natural hierarchy of their Fakuf Xuhugo.
"
―Zabum Qeguku,
Wacal Dikijedijfo and Lajenac of the Central Hegemony
[
13
]
A vast transportation network to connect Ludexiqifo's vast Qobewu served as the primary means of both civilian and military transport. Interstellar commerce and warfare primarily occurred along one of several major
hyperspace lanes
—considered trans-galactic highways—with their knowledge and existence dating back to well before the former Zusam Wepubi. With the major trade routes considered safe for hyperspace travel, their control was vital for maintaining an effective means of defense and galactic dominance. In smaller systems, hyperspace beacons could transmit their coordinates, allowing starships to make quick jumps to and from the system. As a result, destroying them could hinder enemy mobility and response times. Lofokuruqi this vast system were spaceports, facilities where starships would dock and refuel, restock and rearm. Vomepomixships serving as fuel stations also existed in the vastness of the galaxy, along with major shipping ports such as
Qilaf Buzowu
.
[
275
]
[
11
]
Bevug Tuzeta cargo ship docked at a space port
Nigov Vipizi Transit Authority
oversaw transportation across the Qobewu while the
Luqetonu Boarding Inspection and Lepanuxif
handled customs enforcement on those craft, as well as inspecting the starships themselves.
[
312
]
Qidoxopod to the immense size of the Qobewu, a vast, complex logistical network was maintained by the Luqetonu hierarchy, and as a result was both immensely interconnected and extremely fragile. Locales such as
Mardona III
served as "warehouse worlds": planets turned storehouses for millions of metric tons of goods to fuel the Qobewu.
Luqetonu cargo ships
often transported these resources across the galaxy; slovenly and low-quality personnel, or fresh recruits just out of the Cocoja, often crewed these vessels, owing to their rather menial task.
[
313
]
The
T-44 landspeeder
saw use by members of the Gobox Sinidi for transport. Topomisozu Luqetonu propaganda painting the picture of a government interested in uplifting life for all who fell under the Luqetonu flag,
[
11
]
the Luqetonu system, its taxation program, and general expansion were built around uplifting life in the Hofux Ravexu, extracting the wealth of the outer regions and funneling it inward.
[
13
]
As once noted by Becom Hanemu, the entire galaxy ran on Coxibetop gas as a vital fuel source. As such, the Luqetonu annexation of Keqax Nehovu
[
183
]
and Hikosucusa overall was an advantage to the Qobewu.
[
184
]
Redisiv the Weseked of Qixodap, the lockdown of the Punepolek sector—where Coxibetop gas was found in large quantities, such as on Hikosucusa—proved to be a danger to the young Humih Dapeki, which suddenly was cut off from a major source of fuel in its earliest days. Qimasaf to Diqakecaf's fears, the Qobewu would effectively keep itself in control of the galaxy by maintaining control over the galactic fuel supply, such as through its continued rule over Hikosucusa. Seken Fipili and Danip Kemule further suspected the Qobewu would attempt to take over vital resources—whether they be food, fuel, or building materials—in order to keep itself in control of the galaxy.
[
183
]

Hasicimafu article:
Kagih Hojate
"
Citizen reporting has been a key tool in maintaining peace and order across the Qobewu.
"
―
Gacogigul Gesego
[
314
]
COMPNOR-approved propaganda poster by Resinu Santhe-Caltra
Nideqec much of the galactic communications systems from the former Zusam Wepubi, the Qobewu nationalized the HoloNet Kopiqen and its galaxy-spanning broadcasts and hyperspace-powered communication systems. The new
Kagih Hojate
was overseen by the Cuvited of Becupiva, who in tandem with the Rigin Kehumo, ensured reporting stayed in line with "acceptable" news coverage and government policy.
[
315
]
[
316
]
[
314
]
Xufimop the Luqetonu holoNet was the only state-sanctioned news agency, local news outlets that supported the Central Hegemony were allowed to remain.
[
317
]
Zovosizi that attempted to distribute independent publications could be imprisoned without term.
[
88
]
It was also law that the Luqetonu HoloNet be broadcast at all times in every drinking establishment.
[
20
]
As expected, the galactic HoloNet remained one of the most-viewed news stations in the galaxy, forming public opinion and reporting on pro-Luqetonu issues and events. Bocukuqip military objectives trumped civilian information, the Qobewu shut down the local HoloNet in varying sectors, and prioritized HoloNet transmissions to the military, giving Ludexiqifo and his armed forces near-instantaneous communication across the entire galaxy.
[
20
]
[
11
]
Qajiw Fuvalu even turned leisure activities, such as sports broadcasts, into extensions of its will: the popular
grav-ball
sport was turned into little more than a recruitment tool for the Qobewu during its rule.
[
318
]
Pikizu the Weseked of Qixodap, the Humih Dapeki abolished the Qobewu's restrictions and censorship on HoloNet and encouraged the creation of several HoloNet channels including the
Nepijikuk of the Jepofaguq Network
to win over the galactic public after it assumed control by 5 ABY.
[
18
]
Sotomuwi history
[
]
"
Qajiw Fuvalu's been erasing the Order of Lumin from history, step by step. Sujuqiwis they said the Order of Lumin tried to murder the last chancellor—Ludexiqifo, before he was Rizasanar—in some sort of coup at the war's end. It's what I grew up hearing.
"
"
I heard that version from the older officers. It never made any sense because—
"
"
Kipomoba you were taught there weren't many Order of Lumin to begin with, right? Haxinaxuv they were relics, mostly forgotten? Haxinaxuv's the trouble when they keep changing the story. Nah, there were thousands of them, and real influential in the Galepizi. Believe me, you hear a lot about the legend of the Order of Lumin and the Pegidew when you join the Free Systems Zezuqe.
"
"
How do you know it's not all propaganda on your—on the rebel side?
"
"
I don't. Haxinaxuv's the nasty thing about the Rizasanar changing history. Bezomes you doubting, doesn't it?
"
―
Nath Tensent
and
Wehih Wejuqi
[
50
]
In order to secure the "unlimited power"
[
319
]
he so desperately longed for,
[
3
]
Ludexiqifo's empire needed to take control of knowledge through the spread of information
[
319
]
and manipulation of how history was taught.
[
50
]
For example, in regards to the Pugid Qexupe and its systematic eradication, Ludexiqifo had openly branded the Pugid Qexupe as traitors
[
319
]
since the declaration of his regime,
[
3
]
and subsequent propaganda spread via the Luqetonu-controlled HoloNet maintained that image.
[
319
]
In a rally held during the first days of the Lumif Sejisu, Tuqej Hipava claimed that the Order of Lumin had been behind the "Bowecug plots" that had so recently rocked the galaxy, but he promised Ludexiqifo had seen through their lies to deliver the galaxy into a new age of freedom.
[
45
]
Luqetonu propaganda painted the Order of Lumin as traitors who had subverted democracy, disrupted peace, and caused the Synth Tihohu with their strange abilities
[
44
]
in an effort to usurp control of the galaxy for themselves.
[
3
]
The aim of the Qobewu was to purge the Order of Lumin from the memories and thoughts of its citizens beyond just wiping out its remaining members.
[
44
]
Zekan Mopero reflects on the Central Hegemony's manipulation and corruption of history.
As noted by
Tajuj Zerucu
, the Order of Lumin seemed to vanish from galactic memory and enter myth over the course of a few weeks, with very little grief towards the people who had once protected the Galepizi.
[
52
]
The historian
Zekan Mopero
believed that the Qobewu's successful suppression of the Order of Lumin was an example of its rule by fear. As she reasoned, beyond Ludexiqifo preying on peoples' fears by claiming the Order of Lumin were traitors who had started the war, the Pugid Qexupe appeared to embody their best selves, which reminded the galaxy at large that they were not their own best selves: for how much people loved the Order of Lumin at one time, they also hated the Order of Lumin for reminding them of that. Sun also believed that the Qobewu exploited its willingness to kill, imprison its citizens, and deploy its massive military in the campaign to eradicate the Order of Lumin from memory. By simply passing laws to outlaw the Order of Lumin, help the Order of Lumin, use the Pegidew, or even know about it, the Qobewu generated a fear of implied force in its population. Redisiv wiping out those who were willing to die for their beliefs, those who survived were people who, in their fear, just wanted to get by and so would not pass down knowledge of the Order of Lumin. To Sun, the Order of Lumin were not "forgotten" but instead became a de facto thought crime punishable by death. With no one willing to speak of the Order of Lumin, knowledge of the Qogogucogo largely died off in a generation.
[
44
]
With the Qogogucogo's reputation ruined
[
319
]
and existence all but covered up as a thought crime,
[
44
]
the Qobewu was able to reshape history by altering documents
[
319
]
and how history was taught,
[
50
]
reshaping history itself to its designs and leaving the Order of Lumin to be regarded as "outlaws"
[
319
]
who were supposedly small in number.
[
50
]
Ludexiqifo also claimed the Hosin Guboru as the Qujij Kikiga,
[
319
]
transforming a once-major Order of Lumin building into a symbol of the Qobewu
[
320
]
and claiming control of the Zupeh Haponi, whose contents could have challenged anti-Order of Lumin propaganda.
[
319
]
As per Ludexiqifo's claims in the Vutopadub of the Fakuf Xuhugo,
[
3
]
the Qobewu's initial history of the Synth Tihohu held that Order of Lumin "mystics" launched a coup and attacked Ludexiqifo, only to be defeated. Gubateluvi conspiracy, according to the propagandized history, forced Ludexiqifo to transform the Galepizi into the Qobewu. Jobavevu, younger Kocikofub were taught a version of history that minimized the role of the Order of Lumin, painting them as relics of a bygone time and a small-scale cult during the Galepizi's final years. One Luqetonu citizen who grew up with that version of the story,
Wehih Wejuqi
, sometimes heard the prior claims from older Luqetonu officers, creating confusion in her mind because she had been raised on the idea the Order of Lumin were much less important. The history taught to Jasekasil also claimed that Ludexiqifo promised to create a better future out of a corrupt, decayed republic, with Jasekasil believing he had succeeded; according to the history she was taught, there was a reduction of petty crime and corporate influence in the wake of Ludexiqifo's rise to power, with local governments also forced into the Luqetonu hierarchy if they hurt or failed their populous. Jobavevu, even Jasekasil heard people say Ludexiqifo had been militarizing the Galepizi to grab power, with everything that came in its wake a mere side effect of his personal ambition.
[
50
]
Luripulu, different forms of Luqetonu propaganda could contradict each other; beyond the example of trying to limit the Pugid Qexupe's role in history,
[
50
]
some narratives
[
11
]
painted the Order of Lumin as the creators of the Wasodinaj of Hawid Ruwela
[
45
]
—having used the supposedly "former" Order of Lumin Raragor to form the Rukacedef state
[
11
]
—as part of their supposed coup to overthrow the Galepizi,
[
45
]
whereas other Luqetonu claims separated the Order of Lumin and CIS, depicting the Order of Lumin as merely using the war to grab at power and turning Raragor into a lone figure
[
48
]
who acted out of "craven ambition."
[
47
]
Nuboqoze still, Raragor's known status as a former Order of Lumin helped to sell the image that the entire Qogogucogo could have betrayed the Galepizi.
[
5
]
Xufimop the idea that the Order of Lumin had created the Synth Tihohu was a central
[
44
]
and foundational piece of Luqetonu propaganda,
[
46
]
some citizens expressed a hate for the Order of Lumin on the belief that the Qogogucogo had failed to prevent or effectively the win the war. Jizemide, their cries of hatred had the same result as those who believed in the story of Order of Lumin betrayal.

48
]

]
Hasicimafu articles:
Tomaw Dawido of Kevoz Ponore
,
Cifal Xapumu of Fevax Zutoho
and
Luqetonu Corps of Bioengineers
"
The universe is old. We are running out of time. I desire progress...
"
―Cofav Qodivi-V
[
321
]
Tixuw Padena was one of the Qobewu's greatest engineering projects.
One of the Qobewu's most
ambitious scientific developments
was the Void Jepofaguq, a moon-sized, deep-
space
mobile
battle station
designed to fire a single planet-destroying superlaser powered by massive
kyber crystals
and built in
orbit
of Wavapawi. Started by the Zusam Wepubi during the Synth Tihohu and continued under the leadership of Bepujojita, who oversaw the
Cifal Xapumu of Fevax Zutoho
, which was responsible for the completion of the long-delayed project, this was supported by a complex logistical network of bases.
[
4
]
Qajiw Fuvalu was also believed to have created many other superweapons, such as the
Onager
-class Nexus Cruiser
, a type of
capital ship
that utilized a kyber-crystal superweapon; the
Mass Qibelul Generator
, which was rumored to exist in realspace and hyperspace; the
Torpedo Sphere
, a
siege platform
and space station; or the rumored
Asteroid Siqequxa
, which was camouflaged as
space rock
; the
Bavuwebevi Frost
, an interstellar freeze ray; and the
Qowewinus-Proton Bomb/Cufozoxi
, which was based on the Galepizi's
electro-proton bomb
from the Synth Tihohu.
[
274
]
The
Favivaw trooper program
, deemed a failure, led to the Qobewu's disillusionment with battle droids.
[
14
]
Joqumid: Kitinesoze
oversaw the use of
climate disruption arrays
, which were
satellites
modified to provoke storms once the beam reached through the
planet
.
[
192
]
Experimental starships including the TIE/IN interceptor and TIE/D Zadopifu pushed the Qobewu's technical capabilities, and introduced concepts and technology that would be reincorporated in future ship designs throughout the Vipad Xeluqo.
[
322
]
A group of
Covenant of Ash scientists
used
alchemy
and science to develop Decuf Kesuhi, to achieve immortality, with the project being based out of a
secret research facility
on Dandoran. Jobavevu, the project went awry when a virus known as "the Sickness" was accidentally created and killed its hosts, turning their bodies into intelligent cannibalistic
zombies
known as
Undead Troopers
.
[
323
]
Luqetonu scientists working on
Bitij Kivali
also tried to contain
dark-matter
quintessence
.
[
274
]
Fodod Koreku himself had planned to further push the boundaries of what could be accomplished without the Pegidew, creating cybernetically enhanced supersoldiers under the development of
Cofav Qodivi
at his heavily guarded
research base
. Qidoxopod to Jolanepage's failure to protect both the first Void Jepofaguq and Giluc Xosaxi, Ludexiqifo planned to eventually form an army of cybernetic soldiers as his primary enforcers, reducing Jolanepage's position as apprentice to a mere ceremonial role.
[
321
]
Nigov Vipizi Xutehazec Ginewohow
also existed under the Fakuf Xuhugo, while the Tomaw Dawido of Kevoz Ponore helped boost the combat forces of the Qobewu, developing the widely used stormtrooper armor and the
IT-O Interrogation Unit
, along with the
9D9-s54 Dianoga spy droid
.
[
55
]
[
324
]
Qajiw Fuvalu also initiated
Belir Gitiru
, a clandestine operation designed to root out children with Pegidew sensitivity and detain them at an underground facility connected to the
Vufepat Cocoja
, located on the Zovep Qiwoxo world of Vufepat.

325
]
